Let's simulate mentally some algorithms and their expected performance.

1. Multiplicative full variance:
Initialize p from previous best (if available) or random. At each iter:
 c=fft_conv_p
 score=2n max c
 d = c - U (maybe plus peak)
 g=correlate(d,p)
 p *= exp(-η g/gscale)
 normalize.
Because c-U sum zero; total gradient sum = 2∑g_i = 2∑_k d_k (∑p)=2∑d =0, so sum p unchanged up to normalization.

Let's explore Fourier factorization iteration. It might jump to better basin than time-domain gradient. Procedure for length n (M=2n):
1. Given p (first n), embed into length M p_full = [p, zeros M-n] (tail length n? Since M=2n, tail length n, actually p length n, tail n). Wait support n, tail M-n = n. Max sum 2n-2=M-2, so one lag M-1 zero. Good.
2. Compute q = cyclic conv p_full*p_full length M = linear full conv length M (last zero). Use N=M power2.
3. Create target t: uniform 1/(M-1) for 0..M-2, 0 last. Or blend with current q: t = α q + (1-α) ideal? Since ideal infeasible, use α to stay feasible.
4. Compute Q=fft(t). Set P[0]=1 (real positive). For k=1..M/2-1, P[k]=sqrt(Q[k]) principal or sign; P[M-k]=conj(P[k]); P[M/2]=sqrt(Q[M/2]) real choose sign.
5. p_full_new = ifft(P).real.
6. Project: keep first n, clip negatives to 0, maybe clip all, normalize to sum 1.
7. Iterate.

This is like a "square root" projection. It may find nonnegative factor of target. The sign choices crucial. Principal branch may produce p with negative. We can try multiple sign patterns: principal, random signs for sqrt. Since P^2=Q invariant under sign flips. Each sign pattern yields a different p_full. We can choose sign pattern that maximizes sum positive in first n (i.e., projection sum). This is a combinatorial phase problem (2^{M/2} impossible). But random sign flips and gradient can search. We can use stochastic sign: for each k, P[k] = sqrt(Q[k]) * s_k, s_k=±1. Then set Hermitian. p = ifft(P). Projection. Run multiple random sign seeds. This may escape.

Let's design concrete optimization function:
```
def optimize_mirror(p, n, time_budget, rng, mode, eta_base, beta_start, beta_end, kase, reg):
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score = score_prob(p,n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    t=0
    U = 1.0/(2*n-1)
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_budget:
       q = conv_prob(p,n)
       cur = 2*n*np.max(q)
       if cur < best_score: best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy()
       # choose d
       if mode==0: d = q - U
       elif mode==1: d = 1/(1+np.exp(-kappa*(q-U)))
       elif mode==2: beta = lerp; d = softmax(beta*(q-q.max()))
       elif mode==3: topk weights...
       g = grad(p,n,d)
       # normalize g
       gmax = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax<1e-300: break
       g = g/gmax
       eta = eta_base / (1+t*0.001) maybe
       logp = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
       logp -= eta*g
       p_new = np.exp(logp - logp.max())
       p_new += floor
       p_new /= p_new.sum()
       # optional mix uniform
       if reg>0: p_new = (1-reg)*p_new + reg*(1/n)
       if rng.random()<0.01: p_new = add noise
       p = p_new
       t+=1
    return best_p,best_score
```

Spectral initialization function:
```
def spectral_init(n, rng, target_mix, n_iters):
    M=2*n
    p = np.ones(n)/n maybe or random
    # maybe random sign root iterations
    q_ideal = ... Q_ideal array M
    for iter in range(n_iters):
       p_full = np.zeros(M); p_full[:n]=normalize_prob(p)
       P = fft(p_full, M)
       Qcur = P*P
       Q = (1-alpha)*Qcur + alpha*Qideal
       root=P.copy()
       root_new = sqrt(Q)
       sign = real(P*conj(root_new))<0
       root_new[1:M//2]=where(sign, -root_new[1:M//2], root_new[1:M//2])
       root_new[M//2] = sign2*sqrt(abs(Q[M//2]))
       root_new[0]=1
       for k>half: root_new[k]=conj(root_new[M-k])
       p_new_full = ifft(root_new).real
       # take window? just first n
       p = np.maximum(p_new_full[:n],0)
       s=p.sum(); if s<eps: p=np.ones(n)/n else p/=s
    return p
```

`update_log_p(p, g, eta, floor, rng, noise_scale)`:
   g = g - np.mean(g)? Since g may have mean zero for d sum zero; for positive d, mean positive. In log update, constant shift only affects normalization; subtract mean to improve stability. Do `g = g - np.mean(g)`.
   gs = np.max(np.abs(g)); if gs<eps return p.
   logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, floor))
   logp -= eta * g / gs
   # floor log: min_log = np.log(floor)
   logp = np.maximum(logp, min_log)
   logp -= logp.max()
   p2 = np.exp(logp)
   p2 += floor  # maybe double
   s = p2.sum()
   if s <= eps: return uniform
   return p2/s

```
def fft_p_and_q(p, n, N):
    P = fft(p, N)
    c = real(ifft(P*P, N))[:2*n-1]
    return c, P
def fft_grad_from_p(P, d, n, N):
    D=fft(d,N)
    return 2*real(ifft(np.conj(P)*D,N))[:n]
```

Let's define `normalize_prob` top-level:
```
def normalize_prob(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    n = a.size
    if s <= 1e-300 or n==0:
       return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    return a / s
```

`add_noise_log`:
```
def add_noise_log(p, sigma, rng):
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=p.size)
    p2 = p * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -50, 50))
    return normalize_prob(p2)
```

`mix_uniform`:
```
def mix_uniform(p, rho):
    if rho<=0: return p
    u=np.full_like(p,1.0/p.size)
    p2=(1-rho)*p + rho*u
    return normalize_prob(p2)
```

`step_log_update`:
```
def step_log_update(p, g, eta, min_p):
    g = g - np.mean(g)
    gs = np.max(np.abs(g))
    if gs < 1e-300: return p
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, min_p))
    logp = logp - eta * (g/gs)
    logp = np.maximum(logp, np.log(min_p))
    logp = logp - np.max(logp)
    p2 = np.exp(logp)
    p2 += min_p
    s=p2.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return normalize_prob(p)
    return p2/s
```
If eta is negative? Not.

`compute_score_q(q,n)` returns `2*n*max(q)` but q from p sum 1. If q may sum not exactly 1 due to numerical, fine.

```
def select_d(q, mode, t, frac, U, n, rng):
   if mode==0: d=q-U
   elif mode==1:
       kappa = 1e4 * (1e3)**frac? Let's define params.
```
To allow different schedules, pass `kappa_start`, `kappa_end`, `beta_start`, `beta_end`.
```
def make_d(q, mode, frac, U, kappa_start, kappa_end, beta_start, beta_end, top_power_start, top_power_end):
   if mode==0: return q-U
   if mode==1:
      kappa = kappa_start * (kappa_end/kappa_start)**frac
      x = np.clip(kappa*(q-U), -50, 50)
      return 1/(1+np.exp(-x))
   if mode==2:
      beta = beta_start * (beta_end/beta_start)**frac
      t = np.clip(beta*(q - np.max(q)), -700, 0)
      e=np.exp(t)
      s=e.sum()
      if s<=0: d=np.zeros_like(q); d[argmax]=1; else d/e/s
   if mode==3:
      # exponent power: d=(q/qmax)^p; p grows
      qmax=np.max(q)
      ratio = np.maximum(q,0)/qmax
      p = top_power_start + (top_power_end-top_power_start)*frac
      # to avoid underflow, log ratio
      d = np.exp(p * np.log(ratio+1e-300))
      s=d.sum(); d/=s
   return d
```
For mode 3, p=1 gives uniform over q? Actually ratio^1 sum maybe; larger top. Could use p from 1 to 1000. But exponent log underflow; use `np.where(ratio>0, p*np.log(ratio), -1000)`. Since ratio<=1, log <=0. Good.
Maybe mode 2 softmax better.

`def spectral_init(n, rng, n_iters=50, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.3, initial=None):`
- M = get_fft_size(n)? For exact cyclic factor, need M=2n power2. Use N = 2*n if n power2 else next pow2 >=2n? For ideal target with one missing lag at N-1? If N>2n, linear conv has zeros beyond 2n-2 and N-1 not special. Target ideal should be uniform over nonzero lags 0..2n-2, zeros elsewhere. Q_ideal can be computed as fft of that target vector length N. Use ideal = np.zeros(N); ideal[:2*n-1]=1/(2*n-1); Qideal=fft(ideal,N). This works for any N.
- Start p: if initial, use; else ones/n or random.
- For iters:
   p_full = zeros(N); p_full[:n]=normalize_prob(p)
   P=fft(p_full,N)
   Qcur=P*P
   alpha = lerp based on iter
   Q = (1-alpha)*Qcur + alpha*Qideal
   root = np.sqrt(Q)  # principal
   # choose roots closer to P (only for k 1..N/2-1)
   half=N//2
   for vector sign: mask = real(P[1:half]*conj(root[1:half])) < 0; root[1:half]=where(mask,-root[1:half],root[1:half])
   # k=half: if N even, Q[half] real? ideal and p real => Q[half] real. root may be complex small imag. Choose sign: val = abs(np.sqrt(abs(Q[half]))); if real(P[half])<0: val=-val. root[half]=val (real)
   # zero: root[0]=1 (positive)
   # Hermitian: for k=1..half-1, root[N-k]=conj(root[k]); for N odd? N power2 even.
   root_full = np.empty(N, complex)
   root_full[0]=1
   if half>0: root_full[half]=float(real(root[half])) # after sign
   root_full[1:half]=root[1:half]
   root_full[half+1:]=np.conj(root[1:half][::-1])
   p_new_full = np.real(ifft(root_full, N))
   # Project: take first n; maybe if sum small, try shifts? For init, to improve, choose best among a few shifts? But actual sequence can choose start? If we choose a contiguous block of length n from p_new_full that has largest nonnegative sum, then cyclic support may wrap. But this is only initialization for time-domain linear length n; the linear conv of the chosen block will be different from cyclic factor. We can choose first n. To get more positive, we can rotate p_new_full to align positive mass to first n. Does rotating affect? It gives a valid sequence (length n) but the factor relation to ideal shifted maybe. Could help. We can try 4 shifts: 0, n/4, n/2, 3n/4 and choose block of length n with largest sum after clipping positive. For each shift s: `block = np.roll(p_new_full, s)[:n]`; positive sum = sum(max(block,0)); choose max. Then p=normalize(max(block,0)). This may be better. But if block not starting at 0, the linear convolution of this block is not the cyclic conv of the factor; but spectral init just heuristic.
   Use best block with largest positive mass. Keep.
   Optional random sign: if rng.random()<0.1, flip signs for random subset? Tracking P avoids.
- Return p.

Now, `compute_d` implementation efficient:
```
def make_d(q, mode, frac, U):
    q = np.asarray(q,float)
    if mode==0:
       return q - U
    if mode==1:
       c = 20.0 + (1000.0-20.0)*frac
       x = np.clip((c/U)*(q-U), -50, 50)
       return 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-x))
    if mode==2:
       c = 10.0 + (500.0-10.0)*frac
       beta = c / U
       t = beta*(q - np.max(q))
       t = np.clip(t, -700.0, 0.0)
       e = np.exp(t)
       s = e.sum()
       if s <= 1e-300:
           d = np.zeros_like(q); idx=int(np.argmax(q)); d[idx]=1.0; return d
       return e/s
    if mode==3:
       p_power = 2.0 + (1000.0-2.0)*frac
       qmax = np.max(q)
       if qmax <= 0: ...
       ratio = np.maximum(q,0.0)/qmax
       # log ratio; for ratio=0 set -1000
       logd = np.where(ratio > 1e-300, p_power * np.log(ratio), -1000.0)
       logd -= np.max(logd)
       d = np.exp(np.clip(logd, -700, 0))
       s=d.sum()
       if s<=...: top jag
       return d/s
```
For mode1 sigmoid, x positive for q>U. If c/U large, x may be 1000? clip 50. d near 0/1. Good.
For mode2, t max 0; exp okay.
For mode3, if qmax is max, top ratio=1 -> logd=0. Others lower; p_power large underflows.

Let's detail functions:
```
def get_fft_size(n):
    size = 2*n
    if size & (size-1) == 0:
        return size
    return 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
```
If n power2, size=2n power2. If n=4096, yes.
But if n=8192, 16384. Good.

`def fft_p_and_q(p,n,N):`
```
P = fft(p, N)
c = np.real(ifft(P*P, N))
end = 2*n - 1
c = c[:end]
# To remove tiny negative? maybe c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
# But for variance/softmax, negative tiny can affect; clip.
c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0? Use c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
return c, P
```
Clipping c to nonnegative may slightly break sum but good. Since true q nonnegative. If numerical negative large? not.
`P` complex. If P overwritten? no.

`def fft_convolve(p,n,N):`
```
P=fft(p,N); c=np.real(ifft(P*P,N))[:2*n-1]; return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```

`def fft_grad(P,d,n,N):`
```
D=fft(d,N); g=2.0*np.real(ifft(np.conj(P)*D,N))[:n]
return g
```
Need `d` length 2n-1; fft pads zeros to N. Good.

`def normalize_prob(a):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()? maybe no copy input.
a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
a=a.reshape(-1)
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-300 or a.size==0: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size if a.size else np.ones(1)
return a/s
```
If input scalar? size0? not.

`def score_q(q,n): return float(2.0*n*np.max(q))`

`def make_d(q, mode, frac, U):` as above. Ensure `frac` 0..1.
For mode 3, `logd` maybe large negative; do stable:
```
ratio = np.maximum(q,0.0)/qmax
if mode...:
 power = 2+1000*frac
 # use log ratio; for ratio close 1, log 0. To avoid log(0)
 mask = ratio > 1e-300
 logd = np.full_like(q, -1000.0)
 logd[mask] = power * np.log(ratio[mask])
 logd -= np.max(logd)
 e=np.exp(np.clip(logd, -700, 0)); s=e.sum()
 if s<=...: d top
```
This is fine. But if qmax is small (should not), handle.

`def step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p):`
Direction may be `g` or momentum. It can have negative/positive. We'll center and scale inside? To avoid repeated, maybe function centers:
```
def step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p):
    d = direction - np.mean(direction)
    scale = np.max(np.abs(d))
    if scale < 1e-300: return p
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, min_p))
    logp -= eta * d / scale
    logp = np.maximum(logp, np.log(min_p))
    logp -= np.max(logp)
    p2 = np.exp(logp)
    p2 += min_p
    s=p2.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return p
    return p2/s
```
If eta=0 return.

`def add_noise_log(p, sigma, rng):`
```
if sigma<=0: return p
noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, p.size)
noise = np.clip(noise, -50, 50)
p2 = p * np.exp(noise)
return normalize_prob(p2)
```

`def mix_uniform(p, rho):`
```
if rho<=0: return p
rho = min(1.0, max(0.0, rho))
n=p.size
p2=(1-rho)*p + (rho/n)*np.ones(n) # since uniform sum1, rho*1/n
return normalize_prob(p2)
```
Actually `(rho/n)` per entry; sum rho. Good.

`def interpolate_sequence(a, n_new):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(a),0)
if a.size==0: return np.full(n_new,1.0/n_new)
x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
return normalize_prob(out)
```
If a has zeros, interpolation nonnegative.

`def random_lognormal_init(n, rng, smooth_sigma=0.5):`
```
base = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
# optionally smooth using FFT low-pass
# scale
p = np.exp(base)
# add uniform floor
p += 0.01*np.ones(n)
return normalize_prob(p)
```
Maybe add oscillatory to avoid uniform: `base += 0.5*np.sin(2π rng.uniform(1,50)*np.linspace(0,1,n))`. Use random frequencies.
```
def random_pattern_init(n, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   p=1.0
   for _ in range(3):
      f = rng.uniform(1,20)
      amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)
      phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
      p += amp*np.sin(2π*f*x+phase)
   p += rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
   p = np.maximum(p, 0.01)
   return normalize_prob(p)
```
This gives smooth positive.

`def spectral_init(n, rng, n_iters=60, initial=None, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.2, shift_trials=4):`
Implementation top-level. Need avoid closure. Detailed:
```
def spectral_init(n, rng, n_iters=60, initial=None, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.3, shift_trials=8):
    N = get_fft_size(n)  # >=2n-1, hopefully 2n if n power2
    if initial is not None:
        p=normalize_prob(initial)
    else:
        # start random pattern
        p=random_pattern_init(n,rng)
    ideal = np.zeros(N)
    m=2*n-1
    ideal[:m]=1.0/m
    Qideal = fft(ideal, N)
    for it in range(n_iters):
        p_full = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        p_full[:n]=p
        P = fft(p_full, N)
        Qcur = P*P
        alpha = alpha_start + (alpha_end-alpha_start)*(it/max(1,n_iters-1))
        Q = (1.0-alpha)*Qcur + alpha*Qideal
        root = np.sqrt(Q)
        half = N//2
        if half > 1:
            left = root[1:half]
            sign = np.real(P[1:half] * np.conj(left)) < 0.0
            left = np.where(sign, -left, left)
            root[1:half] = left
            root[half+1:] = np.conj(left[::-1])
        if half > 0:
            val = np.real(np.sqrt(np.real(Q[half])))  # could be nan if negative real due to imag
            if np.isfinite(val):
                if np.real(P[half]) < 0: val = -val
                root[half] = val + 0j
            else:
                root[half] = 0j
        root[0] = 1.0 + 0j
        p_full_new = np.real(ifft(root, N))
        # choose best shift block
        best_block = None; best_val=-1
        shifts = [0]
        if shift_trials>1:
            step = max(1,n//shift_trials)
            shifts += [ (s*step) % N for s in range(1, shift_trials) ]
        # But taking block of length n from length N and then actual sequence length n. Rotating by s means block = p_full_new[s:s+n] if s+n<=N else wrap. Use np.roll.
        for s in shifts:
           block = np.roll(p_full_new, s)[:n]
           pos = np.maximum(block,0.0)
           val = pos.sum()
           if val > best_val:
              best_val = val; best_block = pos
        if best_block is None or best_val <= 1e-300:
           p = np.ones(n)/n
        else:
           p = normalize_prob(best_block)
    return p
```
But if N may be >2n (e.g., n not power2), shifts by N/n? n_new? For shifts, using N maybe. If block length n from cyclic length N, if choose shift s, block [s..s+n-1 mod N]. Linear conv of this block not necessarily related, but init. Use `np.roll(p_full_new, s)[:n]` where shift negative? `np.roll(a, s)` moves elements right by s; block from -s? Any contiguous. Good.
Potential issue: `root[half+1:] = np.conj(left[::-1])` if N=2? left length 0. But N large power2 >=2n, n>=16 => N>=32.
Need ensure `root` Hermitian: For N even, index half is Nyquist, should be real. We set. For indices >half excluding half, yes.
`Q[half]` should be real; due to numeric may have small imag. Use `val = np.sqrt(abs(np.real(Q[half])))`. If real negative, sqrt of abs. Sign can be arbitrary; choose based P[half] real. If P[half] complex small, use real.
`np.sqrt(Q)` uses complex; for Q with negative real, root branch. Flipping signs based on P may keep continuity but if Qnear zero, sign may flip randomly. okay.
This spectral_init does not compute actual score; returned p maybe poor. Use as init.

Could we use spectral iteration as an optimization mode with `p` maybe better than gradient? We can call it for initial only.

Maybe improve spectral_init by blending ideal less aggressive and using previous p to choose root. If initial is given high-quality, alpha small, it may preserve and slowly flatten. This could produce a new starting point. We can use it as an in-iteration optimizer? It is deterministic with projection and no line search; might improve if alpha small. But time-domain gradient more flexible.

Now, `optimize_mirror` returns best_p, best_score. Need N inside. Use `N = get_fft_size(n)`.
Pseudo full:
```
def optimize_mirror(p, n, budget, rng, mode=1, eta_base=0.3, momentum=0.9, ls_every=50,
                    eta_min=0.02, noise_prob=0.003, noise_sigma=0.2, reg=0.0,
                    stall_limit=8000, min_p_frac=1e-15, ls_factors=None):
    p=normalize_prob(p)
    N=get_fft_size(n)
    U=1.0/(2.0*n-1.0)
    min_p = min_p_frac / n
    if ls_factors is None: ls_factors = (0.05,0.15,0.4,1.0,2.5)
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+budget
    t=0; last_improve=0; mom=np.zeros(n)
    # initial score
    q,P=fft_p_and_q(p,n,N); cur=score_q(q,n); best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy()
    no_improve_ls=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       tord=time.monotonic()
       if tord + 0.1 > end: break
       t += 1
       frac = min(1.0, (tord-start)/max(budget,1e-6))
       q, P = fft_p_and_q(p,n,N)
       cur = 2.0*n*np.max(q)
       if cur < best_score - 1e-15:
           best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=t
       d=make_d(q, mode, frac, U)
       g=fft_grad(P,d,n,N)
       # normalize and momentum
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       gs = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gs < 1e-300:
           break
       g = g/gs
       if momentum > 0:
           mom = momentum*mom + (1-momentum)*g
           direction = mom - np.mean(mom)
       else:
           direction = g
       ds = np.max(np.abs(direction))
       if ds < 1e-300:
           break
       eta = eta_base * (1.0 - 0.7*frac)
       if eta < eta_min: eta=eta_min
       if ls_every > 0 and (t % ls_every == 0):
           best_cand = None; best_sc = cur
           # include current as candidate
           for fac in ls_factors:
               pc = step_log_update(p, direction, eta*fac, min_p)
               q_c = fft_convolve(pc, n, N)
               sc = 2.0*n*np.max(q_c)
               if sc < best_sc:
                   best_sc = sc; best_cand = pc.copy()
           if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cur:
               p = best_cand; cur = best_sc
               no_improve_ls = 0
               if cur < best_score:
                   best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy(); last_improve=t
           else:
               no_improve_ls += 1
               if no_improve_ls >= 4:
                   # perturb
                   p = mix_uniform(best_p, 0.1)
                   p = add_noise_log(p, 0.4, rng)
                   mom = np.zeros_like(d? n)
                   no_improve_ls=0
       else:
           p = step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p)
           # maybe no score; but if reg/noise
       if reg > 0:
           p = mix_uniform(p, reg)
       if noise_prob > 0 and rng.random() < noise_prob:
           p = add_noise_log(p, noise_sigma, rng)
       if t - last_improve > stall_limit:
           # strong reset around best
           p = mix_uniform(best_p, 0.2)
           p = add_noise_log(p, 0.6, rng)
           mom = np.zeros(n)
           last_improve = t
    # final compute
    q=fft_convolve(p,n,N); sc=score_q(q,n)
    if sc < best_score: best_p=p.copy(); best_score=sc
    return best_p, best_score
```
Need if momentum array length n; after perturbation `mom=np.zeros(n)` not `np.zeros_like(d?`.
In line search, if best_cand improves cur, update. If not, keep p; but we did not update p. Good.
But if line search candidates all worse, we perturb after several fails. Good.
For non-line-search steps, we don't verify score; might worsen cur, but momentum small eta. Periodic line search and best track mitigate.
Maybe we should compute score after fixed step every `score_every` to detect improvement and stall? We compute at loop start, so next iteration. Good.
Stall limit in iterations; if fixed steps worsen many, line search may still no improve. At stall, reset to best + noise. Good.
Potential issue: `last_improve` updated only when best score improves. If no improve for 8000 iters, reset. If 8000 iters * 2ms =16s; okay. Could be longer. Use 3000? For long runs, 3000 enough. Set `stall_limit=5000`.

```
def run_pipeline(p,n,total_budget,rng,shape_frac=0.25, excess_frac=0.30):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0,total_budget)
    N = get_fft_size(n)
    q = fft_convolve(p,n,N); best_s = score_q(q,n); best_p=p.copy()

       if t_excess>0.5: optimize mode=1
```

Let's write each carefully.

`get_fft_size`:
```
def get_fft_size(n):
    size = 2 * int(n)
    if size > 0 and (size & (size - 1)) == 0:
        return size
    return 1 << (2 * int(n) - 1).bit_length()
```
For n=8192, size=16384 power.

`normalize_prob`:
```
def normalize_prob(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s
```

`fft_convolve`:
```
def fft_convolve(p, n, N):
    P = fft(p, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P, N))
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    c = c[:m]
    # numerical cleanup
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Potential if p length not n? ensure p = p[:n] if longer? In functions, p length n.

`fft_p_and_q`: similar but returns P. Clip c.

`fft_grad`:
```
def fft_grad(P,d,n,N):
    D=fft(d,N)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(np.conj(P) * D, N))
    return g[:n]
```

`score_q`:
```
def score_q(q,n):
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(q)))
```

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(p,n):
    n=int(n); N=get_fft_size(n); q=fft_convolve(p,n,N); return score_q(q,n)
```

`make_d` with modes:
Let's define modes:
0 variance: d=q-U
1 excess smooth: c=20+980*frac (κ/U? Wait κ = c / U? Since U~1/(2n), κ = c/U = c*2n. If c=20, κ~327k for n=8192; x=κ(q-U) q-U ~1e-5 =>3, good. c_end=1000 => κ~16M, x~100 clip. Good.)
2 softmax medium: beta = c/U, c=10+490*frac.
4 softmax high: c=100+400*frac.
3 top_power medium: power=2+498*frac.
5 top_power high: power=20+980*frac.
Need avoid division by U if U zero; U positive.
```
def make_d(q, mode, frac, U):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if mode == 0:
        return q - U
    if mode == 1:
        c = 20.0 + (1000.0 - 20.0) * frac
        x = (c / U) * (q - U)
        x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
        return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-x))
    if mode in (2, 4):
        if mode == 2:
            c = 10.0 + (500.0 - 10.0) * frac
        else:
            c = 100.0 + (500.0 - 100.0) * frac
        beta = c / U
        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        t = beta * (q - qmax)
        t = np.clip(t, -700.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(t)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            d = np.zeros_like(q)
            d[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            return d
        return e / s
    if mode in (3, 5):
        if mode == 3:
            power = 2.0 + (1000.0 - 2.0) * frac
        else:
            power = 20.0 + (2000.0 - 20.0) * frac
        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        if qmax <= 1e-300:
            d=np.zeros_like(q); d[0]=1; return d
        ratio = np.maximum(q, 0.0) / qmax
        logd = np.full_like(q, -1000.0)
        mask = ratio > 1e-300
        logd[mask] = power * np.log(ratio[mask])
        logd -= float(np.max(logd))
        e = np.exp(np.clip(logd, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            d=np.zeros_like(q); d[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0; return d
        return e / s
    # default
    d=q - U; return d
```
For mode2, c_end 500 gives beta~8M for n=8192, t for diff 0.25U maybe -? fine.

`step_log_update`: As above. Use `np.log(min_p)` maybe compute each call. Could pass min_log.
```
def step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p):
    if eta <= 0.0:
        return p
    d = direction - np.mean(direction)
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale <= 1e-300:
        return p
    log_min = np.log(min_p)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, min_p))
    logp = logp - eta * (d / scale)
    logp = np.maximum(logp, log_min)
    logp = logp - float(np.max(logp))
    p2 = np.exp(logp)
    p2 += min_p
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return p
    return p2 / s
```

`add_noise_log`:
```
def add_noise_log(p, sigma, rng):
    if sigma <= 0.0:
        return p
    n = p.size
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
    noise = np.clip(noise, -50.0, 50.0)
    p2 = p * np.exp(noise)
    return normalize_prob(p2)
```

`mix_uniform`:
```
def mix_uniform(p, rho):
    if rho <= 0.0:
        return p
    if rho >= 1.0:
        return normalize_prob(p)
    n=p.size
    p2 = (1.0-rho)*p + (rho/n)*np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_prob(p2)
```

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
       return np.full(n_new,1.0/n_new)
    if a.size == n_new:
       return normalize_prob(a)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return normalize_prob(out)
```

`power_init(n, rng, alpha=0.5, osc_amp=0.2)`:
```
def power_init(n, rng, alpha=0.5, osc_amp=0.2):
    idx = np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=float)
    base = idx ** (-alpha)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = base.copy()
    if rng is not None:
       # add a few random sinusoids in log domain
       logp = np.log(base + 1e-300)
       for _ in range(3):
          f = rng.uniform(1.0, 30.0)
          amp = rng.uniform(0.0, osc_amp)
          phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
          logp += amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f*x + phase)
       p = np.exp(logp - np.max(logp))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return normalize_prob(p)
```
If alpha=0.5, base[0]=1. If alpha=0.4 maybe. We can try multiple alpha values: 0.4,0.5,0.6? a>0.5 may have finite n first element large but maybe after normalization; could be okay. Use alpha choices.
`random_pattern_init`:
```
def random_pattern_init(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    p=np.ones(n)
    for _ in range(4):
       f=rng.uniform(1,40); amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.5); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       p += amp*np.sin(2π f x+ph)
    p += rng.normal(0,0.15,n)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.01* p.max())
    return normalize_prob(p)
```
`random_lognormal` maybe not needed.

`spectral_init`: As above but use N power. Need be efficient. Use `n_iters` small. Could skip if time? It is optional.
```
def spectral_init(n, rng, n_iters=50, initial=None, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.25):
    n=int(n); N=get_fft_size(n); m=2*n-1
    if initial is not None:
       p=normalize_prob(initial)
    else:
       p=power_init(n, rng, 0.5, 0.2)
    ideal=np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
    ideal[:m]=1.0/m
    Qideal=fft(ideal,N)
    for it in range(n_iters):
       p_full=np.zeros(N,dtype=float); p_full[:n]=p
       P=fft(p_full,N)
       Qcur=P*P
       frac=it/max(1,n_iters-1)
       alpha=alpha_start + (alpha_end-alpha_start)*frac
       Q=(1.0-alpha)*Qcur + alpha*Qideal
       root=np.sqrt(Q)
       half=N//2
       if half > 1:
           left=root[1:half]
           sign=np.real(P[1:half]*np.conj(left)) < 0.0
           left=np.where(sign, -left, left)
           root[1:half]=left
           root[half+1:]=np.conj(left[::-1])
       if half > 0:
           qh=Q[half]
           mag=np.sqrt(abs(float(np.real(qh))))
           if np.real(P[half]) < 0.0:
               mag = -mag
           root[half]=mag + 0j
       root[0]=1.0+0j
       p_new=true(ifft(root,N))
       # choose shift with max positive sum in first n
       best = None; bestr=-1
       # try 8 shifts
       for s in range(8):
           shift = (s * n) // 8  # because N=2n often; but if N>2n? Use N//8?
           # better shifts [0, N//8, N//4, ...]
           block = np.roll(p_new, shift)[:n]
           pos = np.maximum(block,0.0)
           val=float(pos.sum())
           if val > bestr:
              bestr=val; best=pos
       if best is None or bestr <= 1e-300:
          p = np.ones(n)/n
       else:
          p = normalize_prob(best)
    return p
```
Need shifts: If N=2n, contiguous block length n. Shifts by s*N//8. For s=0, block first n; s=4 shift n gives last n. Good. If N>2n but still >n, okay. Use `np.roll(p_new, shift)[:n]` with positive shift moves right, block starts at -shift? Any contiguous fine. 8 shifts.
This function uses many FFTs; n_iters=30 for 8192 maybe okay.
Could randomize shifts. Good.

`optimize_mirror` details. Need avoid memory leak. Use local variables.
```
def optimize_mirror(p,n,budget,rng,mode=1,eta_base=0.4,momentum=0.9,ls_every=50,eta_min=0.02,noise_prob=0.003,noise_sigma=0.25,reg=0.0,stall_limit=5000,min_p_frac=1e-15,ls_factors=None):
    n=int(n)
    p=normalize_prob(p)
    if budget <= 0:
       q=fft_convolve(p,n,get_fft_size(n)); return p, score_q(q,n)
    N=get_fft_size(n)
    U=1.0/(2.0*n-1.0)
    min_p=min_p_frac / float(n)
    if ls_factors is None:
       if n <= 8192:
          ls_factors=(0.05,0.15,0.4,1.0,2.5)
       else:
          ls_factors=(0.1,0.3,1.0,2.0)
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+float(budget)
    q,P=fft_p_and_q(p,n,N); cur=score_q(q,n)
    best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy()
    mom=np.zeros(n,dtype=float); t=0; ls_fails=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       now=time.monotonic(); if now + 0.05 >= end: break
       t += 1
       frac=min(1.0,(now-start)/float(budget))
       q,P=fft_p_and_q(p,n,N)
       cur=score_q(q,n)
       if cur < best_score - 1e-15:
          best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy()
       d=make_d(q, mode, frac, U)
       g=fft_grad(P,d,n,N)
       g = g - float(np.mean(g))
       gs=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
       if gs <= 1e-300:
          break
       g = g / gs
       if momentum > 0.0:
          mom = momentum * mom + (1.0 - momentum) * g
          direction = mom - float(np.mean(mom))
       else:
          direction = g
       ds=float(np.max(np.abs(direction)))
       if ds <= 1e-300:
          break
       eta = eta_base * (1.0 - 0.7 * frac)
       if eta < eta_min: eta=eta_min
       if ls_every > 0 and (t % ls_every == 0):
           best_cand=None; best_sc=cur
           # optionally if direction likely good, include smaller factors first
           for fac in ls_factors:
               pc = step_log_update(p, direction, eta*fac, min_p)
               qc = fft_convolve(pc, n, N)
               sc = score_q(qc, n)
               if sc < best_sc:
                   best_sc=sc; best_cand=pc.copy()
           if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cur:
               p=best_cand; cur=best_sc; ls_fails=0
               if cur < best_score:
                  best_score=cur; best_p=p.copy()
           else:
               ls_fails += 1
               if ls_fails >= 3:
                   p = mix_uniform(best_p, 0.1)
                   p = add_noise_log(p, 0.4, rng)
                   mom = np.zeros(n)
                   ls_fails=0
       else:
           p = step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p)
       if reg > 0.0:
           p = mix_uniform(p, reg)
       if noise_prob > 0.0 and rng.random() < noise_prob:
           p = add_noise_log(p, noise_sigma, rng)
       if t - best_improve_t > stall_limit: ???
```
Need track best_improve_t variable. Add `last_improve=t` set 0; update when best_score improves. At start last_improve=0.
In line search update, set last_improve=t.
For fixed steps, if next loop improves, update.
Stall:
```
       if t - last_improve > stall_limit:
           p = mix_uniform(best_p, 0.15)
           p = add_noise_log(p, 0.6, rng)
           mom = np.zeros(n)
           last_improve = t
```
But if `last_improve` = 0 and t large for no improvement, resets. Good.
At end:
```
    q=fft_convolve(p,n,N); sc=score_q(q,n)
    if sc < best_score: best_p=p.copy(); best_score=sc
    return best_p,best_score
```
Need ensure `best_improve_t` defined. Use `last_improve=0`.

Potential issue: In fixed step, p may become NaN? `step_log_update` should avoid. But if direction has NaN, gs NaN. Use `np.nan_to_num(g)`.
Add after g: `g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)`.

`run_pipeline`:
```
def run_pipeline(p,n,total_budget,rng,shape_frac=0.25,excess_frac=0.30,mode_peak=2):
    n=int(n); p=normalize_prob(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+float(total_budget)
    N=get_fft_size(n)
    q=fft_convolve(p,n,N); best_s=score_q(q,n); best_p=p.copy()
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remain > 1.0:
       t_shape = shape_frac * float(total_budget)
       t_shape = min(t_shape, remain - 0.5)
       if t_shape > 0.5:
          pc, sc = optimize_mirror(p,n,t_shape,rng,mode=0,eta_base=0.3,momentum=0.9,ls_every=80,noise_prob=0.005,noise_sigma=0.3,reg=0.001)
          if sc < best_s: best_p=pc; best_s=sc
          p=pc # use returned for further (even if not best? maybe use best? Use p from pipeline for continuity; if sc worse, maybe not. But optimize_mirror returns best within; use that.
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remain > 1.0:
       t_excess = excess_frac * float(total_budget)
       t_excess = min(t_excess, remain - 0.5)
       if t_excess > 0.5:
          pc, sc = optimize_mirror(p,n,t_excess,rng,mode=1,eta_base=0.4,momentum=0.9,ls_every=50,noise_prob=0.004,noise_sigma=0.25,reg=0.0)
          if sc < best_s: best_p=pc; best_s=sc
          p=pc
    remain=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if remain > 0.5:
       t_peak = remain - 0.3
       if t_peak > 0.5:
          pc, sc = optimize_mirror(p,n,t_peak,rng,mode=mode_peak,eta_base=0.7,momentum=0.7,ls_every=20,noise_prob=0.002,noise_sigma=0.2)
          if sc < best_s: best_p=pc; best_s=sc
    return best_p,best_s
```

```
def polish_sequence(p,n,budget,rng):
    n=int(n); p=normalize_prob(p)
    N=get_fft_size(n); q=fft_convolve(p,n,N); best_s=score_q(q,n); best_p=p.copy()

       t1=0.6*budget

       if s<best: update; p=pc

       if s<best: update
    # final shorter mode2? if remain > 0? no
```

Could we use a simple power with exponent optimized near 0.5 to get 1.5? We saw one-sided min π/4. But discrete finite n may have endpoint first lag below average, so max might be at s=1 π/4/n? Score approx π/2=1.570. Not enough. Need oscillations.

Maybe a two-term power: p_i = A(i+1)^-0.5 + B (i+n/2)^-? Could reduce plateau. Our random sinusoids may find.

Could we improve initialization by **FFT square-root of target with one-sided ideal**? Target q perhaps q(s)=C for s≤1 and lower s>1. We can spectral_init with ideal uniform but projection to front. The one-sided arcsine's q is flat low half; maybe spectral_init from it with target uniform could move toward better. Good.

Now, any risk that our `spectral_init` with tracking P and Q ideal creates p with negative causing projection to random, not useful? It only initial. OK.

Let's verify `make_d` mode0 variance can have d with large negative at low lags; gradient may increase p contributions to low lags. This may raise endpoint elements, potentially increasing q_max? But optimization line search ensures score not worsen when accepted. Fixed steps may. Good.

Let's write final code in final answer. Need only fenced Python. Double-check syntax.

One concern: `scipy.fft.fft` and `ifft` functions, when passed Python list p? We pass np arrays. Good.
`np.nan_to_num` with keyword `posinf` available in numpy >=1.17; likely. Could use `np.nan_to_num(a)` simpler. But keyword okay. To be safe, use `np.nan_to_num(a, copy=False)`? It accepts nan, posinf, neginf since 1.17. Fine.

Potential issue: In `normalize_prob`, `np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)` returns new array; if a is scalar? We reshape before. Good.

In `spectral_init`, `mag=np.sqrt(abs(float(np.real(qh))))`; if qh real negative tiny, mag. If qh has large imaginary, ignoring. OK.

In `run_pipeline`, `p = pc` after each phase. But if `pc` is not best overall but best for that phase, good. If phase didn't run due time, p remains. Good.

In `polish_sequence`, use `p = pc` only if improved? We can use `p=pc` returned best; if not improved overall but better than its initial? It returns best during phase, so okay. Use p=pc.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing

    f(a) = 2 * n * max(full_convolution(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

The implementation works with probability vectors p (sum p = 1).  For such a
vector, f(p) = 2 n * max(p * p), so all internal objectives are fast FFT
convolutions.  The search combines several global random/spectral/power-law
initializations, full-lag smoothed gradient descent in log-probability space,
line-search polishing, and final peak-focused optimization.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def get_fft_size(n):
    n = int(n)
    size = 2 * n
    if size > 0 and (size & (size - 1)) == 0:
        return size
    return 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_prob(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0 / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def fft_convolve(p, n, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    P = fft(p, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P, N))
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def fft_p_and_q(p, n, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    P = fft(p, N)
    c = np.real(ifft(P * P, N))
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    c = c[:m]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, P


def fft_grad(P, d, n, N):
    D = fft(d, N)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(np.conj(P) * D, N))
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    return g[:int(n)]


def score_q(q, n):
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(q)))


def fast_score(p, n):
    n = int(n)
    N = get_fft_size(n)
    q = fft_convolve(p, n, N)
    return score_q(q, n)


def make_d(q, mode, frac, U):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    if mode == 0:
        return q - U

    if mode == 1:
        c = 20.0 + (1000.0 - 20.0) * frac
        x = (c / U) * (q - U)
        x = np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0)
        return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-x))

    if mode == 2:
        c = 10.0 + (500.0 - 10.0) * frac
        beta = c / U
        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        t = beta * (q - qmax)
        t = np.clip(t, -700.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(t)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            d = np.zeros_like(q)
            d[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            return d
        return e / s

    if mode == 3:
        power = 2.0 + (1000.0 - 2.0) * frac
        qmax = float(np.max(q))
        if qmax <= 1e-300:
            d = np.zeros_like(q)
            d[0] = 1.0
            return d
        ratio = np.maximum(q, 0.0) / qmax
        logd = np.full_like(q, -1000.0, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = ratio > 1e-300
        logd[mask] = power * np.log(ratio[mask])
        logd -= float(np.max(logd))
        e = np.exp(np.clip(logd, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            d = np.zeros_like(q)
            d[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            return d
        return e / s

    return q - U


def step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p):
    if eta <= 0.0:
        return p
    d = direction - float(np.mean(direction))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale <= 1e-300:
        return p
    log_min = float(np.log(min_p))
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, min_p))
    logp = logp - eta * (d / scale)
    logp = np.maximum(logp, log_min)
    logp = logp - float(np.max(logp))
    p2 = np.exp(logp)
    p2 += min_p
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return p
    return p2 / s


def add_noise_log(p, sigma, rng):
    if sigma <= 0.0:
        return p
    n = int(p.size)
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
    noise = np.clip(noise, -50.0, 50.0)
    p2 = p * np.exp(noise)
    return normalize_prob(p2)


def mix_uniform(p, rho):
    if rho <= 0.0:
        return p
    if rho >= 1.0:
        return normalize_prob(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    p2 = (1.0 - rho) * p + (rho / float(n)) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_prob(p2)


def interpolate_sequence(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.full(n_new, 1.0 / float(n_new), dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n_new:
        return normalize_prob(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return normalize_prob(out)


def power_init(n, rng, alpha=0.5, osc_amp=0.2):
    n = int(n)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    base = idx ** (-float(alpha))
    if rng is not None:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        logp = np.log(base + 1e-300)
        for _ in range(3):
            f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 30.0))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, float(osc_amp)))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            logp += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)
        p = np.exp(logp - float(np.max(logp)))
    else:
        p = base.copy()
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return normalize_prob(p)


def random_pattern_init(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(4):
        f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 40.0))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.5))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)
    p += rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, n)
    floor = 0.01 * float(np.max(p))
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    return normalize_prob(p)


def spectral_init(n, rng, n_iters=50, initial=None, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.25):
    n = int(n)
    N = get_fft_size(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1

    if initial is not None:
        p = normalize_prob(initial)
    else:
        p = power_init(n, rng, 0.5, 0.2)

    ideal = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    ideal[:m] = 1.0 / float(m)
    Qideal = fft(ideal, N)

    for it in range(max(1, int(n_iters))):
        p_full = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        p_full[:n] = p
        P = fft(p_full, N)
        Qcur = P * P
        frac = it / float(max(1, n_iters - 1))
        alpha = alpha_start + (alpha_end - alpha_start) * frac
        Q = (1.0 - alpha) * Qcur + alpha * Qideal

        root = np.sqrt(Q)
        half = N // 2

        if half > 1:
            left = root[1:half]
            sign = np.real(P[1:half] * np.conj(left)) < 0.0
            left = np.where(sign, -left, left)
            root[1:half] = left
            root[half + 1:] = np.conj(left[::-1])

        if half > 0:
            qh = Q[half]
            mag = np.sqrt(abs(float(np.real(qh))))
            if np.real(P[half]) < 0.0:
                mag = -mag
            root[half] = mag + 0.0j

        root[0] = 1.0 + 0.0j
        p_new = np.real(ifft(root, N))

        best_block = None
        best_val = -1.0
        for s in range(8):
            shift = (s * N) // 8
            block = np.roll(p_new, shift)[:n]
            pos = np.maximum(block, 0.0)
            val = float(pos.sum())
            if val > best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_block = pos

        if best_block is None or best_val <= 1e-300:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            p = normalize_prob(best_block)

    return p


def optimize_mirror(
    p,
    n,
    budget,
    rng,
    mode=1,
    eta_base=0.4,
    momentum=0.9,
    ls_every=50,
    eta_min=0.02,
    noise_prob=0.003,
    noise_sigma=0.25,
    reg=0.0,
    stall_limit=5000,
    min_p_frac=1e-15,
    ls_factors=None,
):
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    if budget <= 0.0:
        q = fft_convolve(p, n, get_fft_size(n))
        return p, score_q(q, n)

    N = get_fft_size(n)
    U = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
    min_p = float(min_p_frac) / float(n)

    if ls_factors is None:
        if n <= 8192:
            ls_factors = (0.05, 0.15, 0.4, 1.0, 2.5)
        else:
            ls_factors = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0)
    ls_factors = tuple(float(x) for x in ls_factors)

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + float(budget)

    q, P = fft_p_and_q(p, n, N)
    cur = score_q(q, n)
    best_score = cur
    best_p = p.copy()

    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    last_improve = 0
    prev_cur = np.inf
    ls_fails = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 0.05 >= end:
            break

        t += 1
        frac = min(1.0, (now - start) / float(budget))

        q, P = fft_p_and_q(p, n, N)
        cur = score_q(q, n)

        if cur < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = cur
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_improve = t
        if cur < prev_cur:
            last_improve = t

        d = make_d(q, mode, frac, U)
        g = fft_grad(P, d, n, N)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gs = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gs <= 1e-300:
            break
        g = g / gs

        if momentum > 0.0:
            mom = momentum * mom + (1.0 - momentum) * g
            direction = mom - float(np.mean(mom))
        else:
            direction = g

        ds = float(np.max(np.abs(direction)))
        if ds <= 1e-300:
            break

        eta = float(eta_base) * (1.0 - 0.7 * frac)
        if eta < float(eta_min):
            eta = float(eta_min)

        if ls_every > 0 and (t % int(ls_every) == 0):
            best_cand = None
            best_sc = cur
            for fac in ls_factors:
                pc = step_log_update(p, direction, eta * fac, min_p)
                qc = fft_convolve(pc, n, N)
                sc = score_q(qc, n)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_cand = pc.copy()

            if best_cand is not None and best_sc < cur:
                p = best_cand
                cur = best_sc
                ls_fails = 0
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best_p = p.copy()
                if cur < prev_cur:
                    last_improve = t
            else:
                ls_fails += 1
                if ls_fails >= 3:
                    p = mix_uniform(best_p, 0.1)
                    p = add_noise_log(p, 0.4, rng)
                    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    ls_fails = 0
        else:
            p = step_log_update(p, direction, eta, min_p)

        if reg > 0.0:
            p = mix_uniform(p, float(reg))
        if noise_prob > 0.0 and rng is not None and rng.random() < float(noise_prob):
            p = add_noise_log(p, float(noise_sigma), rng)

        prev_cur = cur

        if t - last_improve > int(stall_limit):
            p = mix_uniform(best_p, 0.15)
            p = add_noise_log(p, 0.6, rng)
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            last_improve = t

    q = fft_convolve(p, n, N)
    sc = score_q(q, n)
    if sc < best_score:
        best_p = p.copy()
        best_score = sc
    return best_p, best_score


def run_pipeline(p, n, total_budget, rng, shape_frac=0.25, excess_frac=0.30):
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    total_budget = float(total_budget)
    if total_budget <= 0.0:
        return p, fast_score(p, n)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + total_budget
    N = get_fft_size(n)
    q = fft_convolve(p, n, N)
    best_s = score_q(q, n)
    best_p = p.copy()

    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 1.0:
        t_shape = float(shape_frac) * total_budget
        t_shape = min(t_shape, remain - 0.5)
        if t_shape > 0.5:
            pc, sc = optimize_mirror(
                p, n, t_shape, rng,
                mode=0, eta_base=0.3, momentum=0.7, ls_every=20,
                noise_prob=0.005, noise_sigma=0.3, reg=0.001,
            )
            if sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_p = pc
            p = pc

    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 1.0:
        t_excess = float(excess_frac) * total_budget
        t_excess = min(t_excess, remain - 0.5)
        if t_excess > 0.5:
            pc, sc = optimize_mirror(
                p, n, t_excess, rng,
                mode=1, eta_base=0.4, momentum=0.7, ls_every=12,
                noise_prob=0.004, noise_sigma=0.25,
            )
            if sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_p = pc
            p = pc

    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 0.75:
        t_peak = remain - 0.3
        if t_peak > 0.5:
            pc, sc = optimize_mirror(
                p, n, t_peak, rng,
                mode=2, eta_base=0.6, momentum=0.5, ls_every=10,
                noise_prob=0.002, noise_sigma=0.2,
            )
            if sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_p = pc

    return best_p, best_s


def polish_sequence(p, n, budget, rng):
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    budget = float(budget)
    if budget <= 0.0:
        return p, fast_score(p, n)

    N = get_fft_size(n)
    q = fft_convolve(p, n, N)
    best_s = score_q(q, n)
    best_p = p.copy()

    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget

    if budget > 150.0:
        t1 = 0.55 * budget
        if t1 > 10.0:
            ls1 = 2 if n <= 8192 else 5
            pc, sc = optimize_mirror(
                p, n, t1, rng,
                mode=2, eta_base=0.8, momentum=0.35,
                ls_every=ls1, noise_prob=0.0,
                ls_factors=(0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0),
            )
            if sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_p = pc
            p = pc

    remain = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remain > 1.0:
        t2 = remain - 0.5
        if t2 > 5.0:
            ls2 = 5 if n <= 8192 else 10
            pc, sc = optimize_mirror(
                p, n, t2, rng,
                mode=3, eta_base=0.6, momentum=0.25,
                ls_every=ls2, noise_prob=0.0,
                ls_factors=(0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 2.0),
            )
            if sc < best_s:
                best_s = sc
                best_p = pc

    q = fft_convolve(best_p, n, N)
    final_s = score_q(q, n)
    return best_p, final_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        global_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)

        best_p = None
        best_n = 8192
        best_score = np.inf

        given_p = None
        try:
            given = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            given = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(given, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1000.0)
            if given.size >= 16 and given.sum() > 0.0:
                given_p = normalize_prob(given)
                given_n = int(given_p.size)
                if given_n in (4096, 8192, 16384):
                    s = fast_score(given_p, given_n)
                    if np.isfinite(s):
                        best_p = given_p.copy()
                        best_n = given_n
                        best_score = s
        except Exception:
            given_p = None

        n_main = 8192
        if given_p is not None and given_p.size in (4096, 8192, 16384):
            n_main = int(given_p.size)
        else:
            n_main = 8192

        time_left = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
        main_budget = min(1350.0, max(50.0, time_left - 450.0))

        jobs = []
        if given_p is not None:
            jobs.append((interpolate_sequence(given_p, n_main), 0.50, 0.10))
        else:
            jobs.append((power_init(n_main, rng, 0.5, 0.3), 0.45, 0.30))

        spec_seed = interpolate_sequence(given_p, n_main) if given_p is not None else power_init(n_main, rng, 0.5, 0.2)
        p_spec = spectral_init(n_main, rng, n_iters=35, initial=spec_seed, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.20)
        jobs.append((p_spec, 0.25, 0.30))

        jobs.append((power_init(n_main, rng, 0.5, 0.35), 0.13, 0.30))
        jobs.append((power_init(n_main, rng, 0.35, 0.35), 0.08, 0.30))
        jobs.append((random_pattern_init(n_main, rng), 0.04, 0.30))

        for idx, (init, weight, shape_frac) in enumerate(jobs):
            if time.monotonic() >= global_deadline - 400.0:
                break
            t_job = float(weight) * main_budget
            cap = global_deadline - time.monotonic() - 400.0
            t_job = min(t_job, max(10.0, cap))
            if t_job < 5.0:
                continue

            p_try, s_try = run_pipeline(init, n_main, t_job, rng, shape_frac=shape_frac)
            if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < best_score:
                best_p = p_try.copy()
                best_n = n_main
                best_score = s_try
            print("main job %d n=%d score=%.9f" % (idx, n_main, s_try))

        if best_score > 1.508:
            n_alt = 16384
            time_left = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
            alt_budget = min(280.0, max(0.0, time_left - 220.0))
            if alt_budget > 80.0:
                alt_jobs = []
                if best_p is not None:
                    alt_jobs.append((interpolate_sequence(best_p, n_alt), 0.55, 0.10))
                spec_seed = power_init(n_alt, rng, 0.5, 0.25)
                p_spec_alt = spectral_init(n_alt, rng, n_iters=25, initial=spec_seed, alpha_start=0.05, alpha_end=0.18)
                alt_jobs.append((p_spec_alt, 0.35, 0.30))
                alt_jobs.append((power_init(n_alt, rng, 0.5, 0.35), 0.10, 0.30))

                for init, weight, shape_frac in alt_jobs:
                    if time.monotonic() >= global_deadline - 200.0:
                        break
                    t_job = float(weight) * alt_budget
                    cap = global_deadline - time.monotonic() - 200.0
                    t_job = min(t_job, max(10.0, cap))
                    if t_job < 5.0:
                        continue
                    p_try, s_try = run_pipeline(init, n_alt, t_job, rng, shape_frac=shape_frac)
                    if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < best_score:
                        best_p = p_try.copy()
                        best_n = n_alt
                        best_score = s_try
                    print("alt job n=%d score=%.9f" % (n_alt, s_try))

        time_left = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_p is None:
            best_p = power_init(8192, rng, 0.5, 0.3)
            best_n = 8192
            best_score = fast_score(best_p, best_n)

        if time_left > 25.0:
            final_p, final_s = polish_sequence(best_p, best_n, max(5.0, time_left - 5.0), rng)
            if np.isfinite(final_s) and final_s < best_score:
                best_p = final_p.copy()
                best_score = final_s

        final = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
        final = np.nan_to_num(final, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        s = float(final.sum())
        if s < 0.01 or not np.isfinite(s):
            idx = np.arange(1, 8193, dtype=np.float64)
            final = idx ** (-0.5)
            final /= final.sum()
        else:
            final /= s

        final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
        print("final score estimate=%.9f len=%d" % (best_score, int(final.size)))
        return [float(x) for x in final]

    except Exception:
        idx = np.arange(1, 8193, dtype=np.float64)
        fallback = idx ** (-0.5)
        fallback /= fallback.sum()
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]
```
